## client/predict

### client.predict.pt-kinds

- exports: BossDevRow, BossLogEntry, BossServerMessage, ClientBossDevMessage, ServerBossDevMessage, ServerBossLifeMessage, ServerBossWindowMessage

## common/network/replication

### network.replication.set

`common/src/network/replication/ShipReplicaSet.ts` (601 lines)

- owns: the client's ShipReplicaSet: every other ship's timeline rebuilt from keyframes, control changes, pulls, clamp links and causes, stepped on demand with the shared replicaStep and rewound when news comes late
- needs: common/ships (replicaStep, ReplicaMode), common/sim (StateRing, ReplicaState, sources, pull fields, clamp links), network/clampProtocol types, network/replication/{wire,hooks,rep,pose,debris}
- invariants:
  - a client rebuilds every other ship exactly from keyframes, control changes and causes against the server's state — [server/src/net/replication.test.ts](../../server/src/net/replication.test.ts)
  - a far ship costs nothing; coming into focus it is sent on its own and leaving is told once — [server/src/net/replication.test.ts](../../server/src/net/replication.test.ts)
  - clamped pairs step as the server steps them — [common/src/network/clampReplicas.test.ts](../../common/src/network/clampReplicas.test.ts)
- extend: a new thing a replica is moved by is a source or cause passed in (hooks.ts), not a branch in its step
- exports: ShipReplicaSet
- sections: NO_PULLS 30-30; PREV 32-33; ShipReplicaSet 35-601

- extend: what a piece asks of the client (its shape, life, rebuild point) is a DebrisWorld member (hooks.ts)
- exports: DebrisSteps
- sections: DebrisSteps 20-88

### lobby.rankings.board

  - a pilot leaves docked at the station they came from with their own ship as it was, by the door, or on a disconnect; dying there is no way out: the respawn is at the door in the same cheat ship and weapon; nobody but a visitor stays in the range — [server/src/game/gunRange/gunRange.test.ts](../../server/src/game/gunRange/gunRange.test.ts)

## server/update

### server.update.countdown

`server/src/update/UpdateCountdown.ts` (116 lines)

- owns: the countdown to a scheduled server update: announcements at 15 / 5 / 1 minutes, the join block in the last minute, the save and clean exit at the time
- needs: an UpdateHost (clock, say, banner, exit); learns the schedule from the lobby's heartbeat answer
- invariants:
  - each of 15 / 5 / 1 minutes is announced once, a late schedule announces only what is still ahead — [server/src/update/countdown.test.ts](../../server/src/update/countdown.test.ts)
  - new sign-ins are refused from 1 minute before — [server/src/update/countdown.test.ts](../../server/src/update/countdown.test.ts)
  - at the time it exits once, through the host (which saves everyone first) — [server/src/update/countdown.test.ts](../../server/src/update/countdown.test.ts)
  - a schedule that is gone from the lobby's answer is a cancellation; a failed heartbeat changes nothing — [server/src/update/countdown.test.ts](../../server/src/update/countdown.test.ts)
- extend: another warning is one more number in MILESTONES_MS
- exports: BANNER_EVERY_MS, JOIN_BLOCK_MS, JOIN_REFUSED, MILESTONES_MS, UPDATE_EXIT_CODE, UpdateCountdown, UpdateHost, span
